```ts
    expect(wrong.map((o) => o.id)).toEqual([]);
    expect(m.objects.get("dining_table_978b20c7")!.room).toBe("living");
  });
});
```

`ui/src/twin3d/core/coords.ts`
```ts
// 평면도(scene plan, 미터)와 three 월드 좌표. 평면 [x, y] = three (x, 높이, y). facing 0 = +x, 90 = +y.
import type { EyePose, Plan2, Vec3 } from "../../../../src/contract/events.ts";

const DEG = Math.PI / 180;
export const planToWorld = ([x, y]: Plan2, h = 0): Vec3 => [x, h, y];
export const worldToPlan = ([x, , z]: Vec3): Plan2 => [x, z];
export const facingToYaw = (facingDeg: number) => (90 - facingDeg) * DEG;
export function yawToFacing(yaw: number): number {
  const d = (((90 - yaw / DEG) % 360) + 360) % 360;
  return Math.abs(d - 360) < 1e-9 ? 0 : d;
}

/** 폰 눈 자세 → 위치·바라보는 방향. 숫자가 하나라도 아니면 null. */
export function eyeToWorld(e: EyePose): { origin: Vec3; dir: Vec3 } | null {
  if (![...e.position, e.height_m, e.yaw_deg, e.pitch_deg].every(Number.isFinite)) return null;
  const y = e.yaw_deg * DEG;
  const p = e.pitch_deg * DEG;
  return { origin: [e.position[0], e.height_m, e.position[1]], dir: [Math.cos(y) * Math.cos(p), Math.sin(p), Math.sin(y) * Math.cos(p)] };
}
```

`ui/src/twin3d/core/home-index.ts`
```ts
// scene.json → 방·물건 색인. 위치 → 방(src/contract/rooms.ts 하나), 물건 → 방, 이웃 방. 카메라 맞춤·Semantic Zoom·에이전트 리듀서가 같이 쓴다.
import type { Plan2, Vec3 } from "../../../../src/contract/events.ts";
import { roomAtIn } from "../../../../src/contract/rooms.ts";
import type { Provenance, RoomKind, SceneJson } from "../../../../src/contract/scene.ts";

export { distanceToPolygon, insidePolygon, ROOM_SNAP_M } from "../../../../src/contract/rooms.ts";

export interface RoomInfo { id: string; label: string; kind: RoomKind | null; polygon: Plan2[]; interior: Plan2[] | null; center: Plan2; area: number; floorZ: number; neighbors: string[]; bounds: { min: Plan2; max: Plan2 } }
export interface ObjectInfo { id: string; type: string; label: string; room: string | null; plan: Plan2; position: Vec3; category: string; mount: string; interactive: boolean; device: boolean; provenance: Provenance | null }
export interface HomeIndex {
  rooms: Map<string, RoomInfo>; roomList: RoomInfo[]; objects: Map<string, ObjectInfo>;
  ceiling: number; cut: number; bounds: { min: Plan2; max: Plan2 };
  roomAt(p: Plan2): string | null; roomOf(objectId: string): string | null;
}

const boundsOf = (pts: Plan2[]) => ({
  min: [Math.min(...pts.map((p) => p[0])), Math.min(...pts.map((p) => p[1]))] as Plan2,
  max: [Math.max(...pts.map((p) => p[0])), Math.max(...pts.map((p) => p[1]))] as Plan2,
});
const areaOf = (pts: Plan2[]) => Math.abs(pts.reduce((a, [x, y], i) => { const [x2, y2] = pts[(i + 1) % pts.length]!; return a + x * y2 - x2 * y; }, 0)) / 2;

export function homeIndexFrom(scene: SceneJson): HomeIndex {
  const neighbors = new Map<string, Set<string>>(scene.plan.rooms.map((r) => [r.id, new Set<string>()]));
  const link = (a?: string, b?: string) => { if (a && b && a !== b && neighbors.has(a) && neighbors.has(b)) { neighbors.get(a)!.add(b); neighbors.get(b)!.add(a); } };
  for (const d of scene.plan.doors ?? []) link(d.connects[0], d.connects[1]);
  for (const [a, b] of scene.plan.open_edges ?? []) link(a, b);
  const roomList: RoomInfo[] = scene.plan.rooms.map((r) => {
    const b = boundsOf(r.polygon);
    return {
      id: r.id, label: r.label, kind: r.kind ?? null, polygon: r.polygon, interior: r.interior ?? null,
      center: r.center ?? [(b.min[0] + b.max[0]) / 2, (b.min[1] + b.max[1]) / 2],
      area: r.area ?? areaOf(r.polygon), floorZ: r.floor_z ?? 0,
      neighbors: r.neighbors ?? [...neighbors.get(r.id)!].sort(), bounds: b,
    };
  });
  const rooms = new Map(roomList.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
  const roomAt = (p: Plan2): string | null => roomAtIn(roomList, p);
  const objects = new Map<string, ObjectInfo>();
  for (const o of scene.objects) {
    if (o.category === "structure") continue;
    objects.set(o.id, {
      id: o.id, type: o.type, label: o.name, room: o.room, plan: o.plan.position, position: o.position,
      category: o.category, mount: o.mount, interactive: o.interactive,
      device: Object.keys(o.attrs ?? {}).length > 0, provenance: o.provenance ?? null,
    });
  }
  return {
    rooms, roomList, objects,
    ceiling: scene.plan.ceiling_height, cut: scene.plan.wall_cut_height,
    bounds: boundsOf(roomList.flatMap((r) => r.polygon)),
    roomAt,
    roomOf: (id) => { const o = objects.get(id); return o ? o.room ?? roomAt(o.plan) : null; },
  };
}
```

`src/contract/rooms.ts`
```ts
// 위치 → 방 규칙 하나. 서버 planner(src/plan/floor.ts)와 화면 HomeIndex가 같이 쓴다. 안쪽 바닥 → 더 깊이 든 중심선 다각형 → 0.15m 안 가까운 방.
import type { Plan2 } from "./events.ts";

export const ROOM_SNAP_M = 0.15;

export function insidePolygon([x, y]: Plan2, poly: Plan2[]): boolean {
  let inside = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = poly.length - 1; i < poly.length; j = i++) {
    const [xi, yi] = poly[i]!;
    const [xj, yj] = poly[j]!;
    if (yi > y !== yj > y && x < ((xj - xi) * (y - yi)) / (yj - yi) + xi) inside = !inside;
  }
  return inside;
}

export function distanceToPolygon([x, y]: Plan2, poly: Plan2[]): number {
  let best = Infinity;
  for (let i = 0, j = poly.length - 1; i < poly.length; j = i++) {
    const [ax, ay] = poly[j]!;
    const [bx, by] = poly[i]!;
    const dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay;
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((x - ax) * dx + (y - ay) * dy) / (dx * dx + dy * dy || 1)));
    best = Math.min(best, Math.hypot(x - (ax + dx * t), y - (ay + dy * t)));
  }
  return best;
}

export function roomAtIn(rooms: { id: string; polygon: Plan2[]; interior?: Plan2[] | null }[], p: Plan2, snap = ROOM_SNAP_M): string | null {
  const inner = rooms.find((r) => r.interior && insidePolygon(p, r.interior));
  if (inner) return inner.id;
  const deep = rooms.filter((r) => insidePolygon(p, r.polygon)).map((r) => ({ id: r.id, d: distanceToPolygon(p, r.polygon) }));
  if (deep.length) return deep.sort((a, b) => b.d - a.d)[0]!.id;
  const near = rooms.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, d: distanceToPolygon(p, r.polygon) })).sort((a, b) => a.d - b.d)[0];
  return near && near.d <= snap ? near.id : null;
}
```

`core/index-store.ts`: 처음 scene으로 `homeIndexFrom`, `apply(layout)`마다 scene 물건 목록에 move·add·remove를 반영하고 새 HomeIndex·판 번호를 만든 뒤 구독자에게 알린다. 시험(`home-index.test.ts`에 더함): add 뒤 `get().objects.has(새 id)`가 참이고 `version()`이 1 오름, move 뒤 `roomOf`가 새 방.
`core/scene-caps.ts`: 구조 아닌 물건 하나라도 `provenance`가 있으면 `provenance`, 방이 모두 `interior`를 가지면 `roomExtras`, 곧은 벽 하나라도 `spans`가 있으면 `spans`. 시험: 고정 자료는 셋 다 참, provenance·interior를 뺀 사본은 거짓.
`src/layout/editor.ts`: 위 Interfaces의 provenance. `tests/layout-editor.test.ts`에 add → `provenance.origin === "runtime"`(모델 없으면 `model === "placeholder"`), move → `origin`·`facing`이 `"manual"`.

`src/contract/scene.ts`: `src/layout/editor.ts`의 `SceneObject`·`ScenePlace`·`LayoutMessage`를 그대로 옮기고 `provenance?: Provenance`를 더한다. 나머지 타입은 위 Interfaces 그대로(필드는 scene.json 실제 키). `editor.ts`는 `export type { SceneObject, ScenePlace, LayoutMessage } from "../contract/scene.js"`. `events.ts`: `import type { LayoutMessage } from "./scene.js"`, `ServerMessage`에 `| LayoutMessage`. `messages.ts` KNOWN에 `"layout"`. `tests/contract.test.ts`에 `src/contract/scene.ts`도 「타입만」 검사, `ui/tests/messages.test.ts`에 `parseMessage('{"type":"layout","op":"remove","id":"x"}')?.type === "layout"`.

`ui/tests/twin3d/fit.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { fitView, viewBasis } from "../../src/twin3d/camera/fit";

const vp = (insets = { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 }) => ({ width: 800, height: 400, insets });
const lim = { minViewHeight: 0.5, maxViewHeight: 100 };
const box = (x1: number, y1: number) => [[0, 0, 0], [x1, 0, 0], [0, y1, 0], [x1, y1, 0]] as [number, number, number][];
const close = (v: number[], w: number[]) => v.forEach((x, i) => expect(x).toBeCloseTo(w[i]!, 3));

describe("fitView", () => {
  const side = viewBasis(0, 90); // +z에서 수평으로 봄: right +x, up +y
  it("4×2 상자를 800×400에 꽉: zoom = 20/2", () => {
    const f = fitView(box(4, 2), side, vp(), 20, 0, lim);
    close(f.target, [2, 1, 0]);
    expect(f.zoom).toBeCloseTo(10);
    close(f.offset, [0, 0]);
  });
  it("오른쪽 200px가 가려지면 남은 600px 가운데로", () => {
    const f = fitView(box(4, 2), side, vp({ top: 0, right: 200, bottom: 0, left: 0 }), 20, 0, lim);
    expect(f.viewHeight).toBeCloseTo(2.6667);
    expect(f.zoom).toBeCloseTo(7.5);
    close(f.offset, [0.6667, 0]);
  });
  it("작은 물체는 최소 보기 높이에서 멈춘다(극단 확대 금지)", () => {
    const f = fitView(box(0.2, 0.2), side, vp(), 20, 0.15, { minViewHeight: 2.5, maxViewHeight: 40 });
    expect(f.viewHeight).toBeCloseTo(2.5);
    expect(f.zoom).toBeCloseTo(8);
  });
  it("가운데는 무게중심이 아니라 투영 범위의 가운데", () => {
    const f = fitView([[0, 0, 0], [0, 2, 0], [4, 0, 0]], side, vp(), 20, 0, lim);
    close(f.target, [2, 1, 0]);
  });
  it("45° 내려다보기: 바닥 정사각형의 target은 바닥 위 가운데", () => {
    const f = fitView([[0, 0, 0], [4, 0, 0], [0, 0, 4], [4, 0, 4]], viewBasis(0, 45), vp(), 20, 0, lim);
    close(f.target, [2, 0, 2]);
  });
  it("여백 margin은 쓸 수 있는 화면을 줄인다", () => {
    const f = fitView(box(4, 2), side, vp(), 20, 0.1, lim);
    expect(f.viewHeight).toBeCloseTo(2.5);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { includeFor, objectPoints, roomPoints } from "../../src/twin3d/camera/targets";

const room = { id: "A", label: "A", kind: null, polygon: [[0, 0], [4, 0], [4, 4], [0, 4]], interior: null, center: [2, 2], area: 16, floorZ: -0.1, neighbors: [], bounds: { min: [0, 0], max: [4, 4] } } as never;

describe("맞출 점", () => {
  it("방: 꼭짓점 × (바닥, 바닥+잘라 보기) + 세운 벽 끝 × 천장", () => {
    const pts = roomPoints(room, 1.05, 2.3, [{ a: [0, 0], b: [4, 0] }]);
    expect(pts).toHaveLength(4 * 2 + 2);
    expect(Math.min(...pts.map((p) => p[1]))).toBeCloseTo(-0.1);
    expect(pts.filter((p) => p[1] === 2.3)).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(pts.filter((p) => Math.abs(p[1] - 0.95) < 1e-9)).toHaveLength(4);
  });
  it("물체: 상자를 옆으로 0.6m 넓히고 함께 볼 점(바닥)을 더한다", () => {
    const pts = objectPoints({ min: [1, 0, 1], max: [2, 1, 2] }, [[5, 5]]);
    expect(Math.min(...pts.map((p) => p[0]))).toBeCloseTo(0.4);
    expect(pts.some((p) => p[0] === 5 && p[2] === 5 && p[1] === 0)).toBe(true);
  });
  it("함께 볼 눈: 같은 방의 맞춘 눈만", () => {
    expect(includeFor("kitchen", { position: [10, 2], room: "kitchen", estimated: false })).toEqual([[10, 2]]);
    expect(includeFor("kitchen", { position: [10, 2], room: "living", estimated: false })).toEqual([]);
    expect(includeFor("kitchen", { position: [10, 2], room: "kitchen", estimated: true })).toEqual([]);
    expect(includeFor("kitchen", null)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`ui/src/twin3d/camera/fit.ts`
```ts
// Fit to Selection: 실제 점을 지금 카메라 방향으로 투영하고, 패널이 가린 부분을 뺀 화면에 여백을 두고 맞춘다.
// 정사영이라 확대는 zoom(= frustumHeight / 보이는 세로 m)으로만 한다. 위치를 옮겨 확대하지 않는다.
import type { Vec3 } from "../../../../src/contract/events.ts";

export interface ViewBasis { right: Vec3; up: Vec3; back: Vec3 }
export interface Viewport { width: number; height: number; insets: { top: number; right: number; bottom: number; left: number } }
export interface FitLimits { minViewHeight: number; maxViewHeight: number }
export interface FitResult { target: Vec3; zoom: number; offset: [number, number]; viewHeight: number }

export const FRUSTUM_HEIGHT = 20;
export const FIT = {
  overview: { margin: 0.02, minViewHeight: 4, maxViewHeight: 40 },
  room: { margin: 0.08, minViewHeight: 3.5, maxViewHeight: 40 },
  object: { margin: 0.15, minViewHeight: 2.5, maxViewHeight: 40 },
} as const;

const DEG = Math.PI / 180;
const dot = (a: Vec3, b: Vec3) => a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1] + a[2] * b[2];
const cross = (a: Vec3, b: Vec3): Vec3 => [a[1] * b[2] - a[2] * b[1], a[2] * b[0] - a[0] * b[2], a[0] * b[1] - a[1] * b[0]];
const scale = (a: Vec3, k: number): Vec3 => [a[0] * k, a[1] * k, a[2] * k];
const add = (a: Vec3, b: Vec3): Vec3 => [a[0] + b[0], a[1] + b[1], a[2] + b[2]];
const norm = (a: Vec3): Vec3 => scale(a, 1 / Math.hypot(...a));

export function viewBasis(azimuthDeg: number, polarDeg: number): ViewBasis {
  const t = azimuthDeg * DEG;
  const p = polarDeg * DEG;
  const back: Vec3 = [Math.sin(p) * Math.sin(t), Math.cos(p), Math.sin(p) * Math.cos(t)];
  const right = norm(cross([0, 1, 0], back));
  return { right, up: cross(back, right), back };
}

export function fitView(points: Vec3[], basis: ViewBasis, vp: Viewport, frustumHeight: number, margin: number, limits: FitLimits, pivotY?: number): FitResult {
  if (!points.length) throw new Error("맞출 점이 없어요");
  let r0 = Infinity, r1 = -Infinity, u0 = Infinity, u1 = -Infinity, depth = 0, minY = Infinity;
  for (const p of points) {
    const r = dot(p, basis.right);
    const u = dot(p, basis.up);
    r0 = Math.min(r0, r); r1 = Math.max(r1, r); u0 = Math.min(u0, u); u1 = Math.max(u1, u);
    depth += dot(p, basis.back);
    minY = Math.min(minY, p[1]);
  }
  const usableW = Math.max(1, vp.width - vp.insets.left - vp.insets.right);
  const usableH = Math.max(1, vp.height - vp.insets.top - vp.insets.bottom);
  const raw = Math.max((r1 - r0) / (usableW * (1 - 2 * margin)), (u1 - u0) / (usableH * (1 - 2 * margin))) * vp.height;
  const viewHeight = Math.min(limits.maxViewHeight, Math.max(limits.minViewHeight, raw));
  const mPerPx = viewHeight / vp.height;
  const base = add(scale(basis.right, (r0 + r1) / 2), scale(basis.up, (u0 + u1) / 2));
  const y = pivotY ?? minY;
  const t = Math.abs(basis.back[1]) > 1e-6 ? (y - base[1]) / basis.back[1] : depth / points.length;
  const target = add(base, scale(basis.back, t));
  const dx = vp.insets.left + usableW / 2 - vp.width / 2;
  const dy = vp.insets.top + usableH / 2 - vp.height / 2;
  return { target, zoom: frustumHeight / viewHeight, offset: [-dx * mPerPx, dy * mPerPx], viewHeight };
}
```

`ui/src/twin3d/camera/targets.ts`
```ts
// 맞출 점: 낮춘 벽은 잘라 보기 높이까지, 세운 벽은 천장까지(레퍼런스처럼 아래쪽 여백이 남지 않게).
import type { Plan2, Vec3 } from "../../../../src/contract/events.ts";
import type { HomeIndex, RoomInfo } from "../core/home-index";

type Raised = { a: Plan2; b: Plan2 }[];

export function roomPoints(room: RoomInfo, cut: number, ceiling: number, raised: Raised): Vec3[] {
  const pts: Vec3[] = [];
  for (const [x, y] of room.polygon) pts.push([x, room.floorZ, y], [x, room.floorZ + cut, y]);
  for (const w of raised) pts.push([w.a[0], ceiling, w.a[1]], [w.b[0], ceiling, w.b[1]]);
  return pts;
}

export function housePoints(index: HomeIndex, raised: Raised): Vec3[] {
  return [...index.roomList.flatMap((r) => roomPoints(r, index.cut, index.ceiling, [])), ...raised.flatMap((w) => [[w.a[0], index.ceiling, w.a[1]], [w.b[0], index.ceiling, w.b[1]]] as Vec3[])];
}

export function objectPoints(box: { min: Vec3; max: Vec3 }, include: Plan2[], padM = 0.6): Vec3[] {
  const pts: Vec3[] = [];
  for (const x of [box.min[0] - padM, box.max[0] + padM]) for (const y of [box.min[1], box.max[1]]) for (const z of [box.min[2] - padM, box.max[2] + padM]) pts.push([x, y, z]);
  for (const [x, z] of include) pts.push([x, 0, z]);
  return pts;
}

/** 물체 맞춤에 함께 넣을 점: 같은 방의 맞춘 눈 위치만(추정 눈·다른 방은 넣지 않음). */
export function includeFor(roomId: string | null, cloid: { position: Plan2; room: string | null; estimated: boolean } | null): Plan2[] {
  return cloid && !cloid.estimated && cloid.room && cloid.room === roomId ? [cloid.position] : [];
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/lib/event-bus.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/render/frame-plan.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/render/TwinEngine.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/render/create-engine.ts`(실제 WebGLRenderer·합성기 만들기), `ui/src/twin3d/scene/registry.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/scene/build.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/scene/loaders.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/scene/TwinScene.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/TwinRuntime.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/ui/TwinCanvas.tsx`, `ui/src/twin3d/ui/TwinData.tsx`, `ui/src/twin3d/Twin3d.tsx`, `ui/src/twin3d/port.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/iframe-port.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/debug.ts`, `ui/public/twin3d/basis/*`(three `examples/jsm/libs/basis` 복사), 시험 `ui/tests/event-bus.test.ts`, `ui/tests/twin3d/frame-plan.test.ts`, `engine.test.ts`, `build.test.ts`, `twin-scene.test.ts`, `twin-canvas.test.tsx`, `twin-data.test.tsx`, `runtime.test.ts`, `boundaries.test.ts`
- Modify: `ui/src/dashboard/Dashboard.tsx`(`useEventBus()`로 소켓 하나, `EventBusContext`·`TwinDataProvider`로 감쌈, `?twin=three`이면 `TwinStage` 대신 `Twin3d`, 기본은 아직 iframe), `CLAUDE.md`(규칙 표: 의존 방향 한 줄)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SceneJson`, `IndexStore`·`sceneCaps`(Task 5), `applyTransform/assetRoot/uniqueAssets/indexNodes/isPlaceholder`(Task 7), `boxSpec/routeLayout/movePatch/normalizeObject`(Task 7), `createLiveSync/createContext/applyState/collectTargets`(Task 6)
- Produces:
  - `class EventBus { push(m: ServerMessage): void; connection(open: boolean): void; open(): boolean; subscribe(onMessage: (m: ServerMessage, replay: boolean) => void, onOpen?: (open: boolean) => void): () => void }`. 스냅숏: 마지막 `hello`, 기기별 마지막 `state`, 마지막 `hello` 뒤의 `layout` 차례대로, 마지막 `eye`·`plan`. 새 구독자는 스냅숏을 `replay: true`로 먼저 받는다. `hello`가 오면 `state`·`layout`을 비운다(서버가 다시 보냄). `hello.boot`가 바뀌면 `eye`·`plan`도 비운다.
  - `useEventBus(): EventBus`(Dashboard에서 한 번, `useEvents`를 감쌈. 콜백은 `useCallback`으로 고정), `EventBusContext`
  - `<TwinDataProvider bus>`: `/twin/scene.json`을 한 번 받아 `{ scene: SceneJson; index: IndexStore; caps; base: "/twin/" } | null`을 context로. 버스의 `layout`을 `index.apply`. 3D 경로와 상관없이 Dashboard에 늘 있다(Task 32에서 AgentHub를 더함). `useTwinData()`
  - `type DirtyReason = "load" | "resize" | "walls" | "state" | "layout" | "preset" | "selection" | "overlay" | "camera" | "highlight"`
  - `interface FrameFlags { moving: boolean; transitioning: boolean; spinning: boolean; animating: boolean; dirty: ReadonlySet<DirtyReason> }`, `planFrame(f: FrameFlags): { render: boolean; base: boolean; shadow: boolean; continuous: boolean }`(base = 본 장면을 다시 그림. 오버레이만 바뀌었거나 오버레이 움직임만 있으면 false)
  - `interface RendererLike { setPixelRatio(r: number): void; setSize(w: number, h: number, style: boolean): void; dispose(): void; forceContextLoss(): void; shadowMap: { needsUpdate: boolean }; info: { autoReset: boolean; reset(): void; render: { calls: number; triangles: number }; memory: { textures: number; geometries: number } } }`
  - `interface ComposerLike { setPixelRatio(r: number): void; setSize(w: number, h: number): void; render(dt: number, opts: { base: boolean }): void; dispose(): void }`(dispose는 모든 패스와 렌더 타깃을 지움)
  - `interface EngineDeps { createRenderer(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement): RendererLike; createComposer(r: RendererLike, e: TwinEngine): ComposerLike; raf(cb: (t: number) => void): number; caf(id: number): void }`
  - `class TwinEngine { static live: number; scene: Scene; overlay: Scene; camera: OrthographicCamera; renderer: RendererLike; frames: number; constructor(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, deps: EngineDeps, opts: { dprMax: number; frustumHeight: number; animFps?: number | null }); resize(w: number, h: number, dpr: number): void; invalidate(r: DirtyReason): void; setActivity(k: "moving" | "transitioning" | "spinning" | "animating", on: boolean): void; onBeforeRender(cb: (dt: number) => void): () => void; onAfterRender(cb: () => void): () => void; setComposer(c: ComposerLike): void; lastFrame(): { calls: number; triangles: number }; viewport(): { width: number; height: number; dpr: number }; dispose(): void }`(`live` = 지금 살아 있는 엔진 수 = WebGL 문맥 수)
  - `class Registry { add(id: string, node: Object3D, entry: SceneObject): void; remove(id: string): void; node(id: string): Object3D | null; entry(id: string): SceneObject | null; ids(): string[]; solidBox(id: string): Box3 | null; objectOf(o: Object3D): string | null; roomOfFloor(o: Object3D): string | null; wallOf(o: Object3D): { wall: string; span: number | null; rooms: string[]; part: "lower" | "upper" } | null; wallPieces(): { key: string; wall: string; kind: "exterior" | "interior" | "solid"; faces: WallFace[]; span: number | null; rooms: string[]; lower: Object3D[]; upper: Object3D[] }[] }`(`kind`·`faces`는 벽 holder의 `userData.wall` JSON, `rooms`는 조각 extras, 나뉘지 않은 벽은 `faces`의 방 전부)
  - `interface AssetSource { load(url: string): Promise<Object3D> }`, `buildScene(scene: SceneJson, src: AssetSource, baseUrl: string): Promise<{ root: Group; registry: Registry; placeholders: string[] }>`
  - `class TwinScene { ready: boolean; registry: Registry | null; constructor(engine: TwinEngine, src: AssetSource, index: IndexStore); load(scene: SceneJson, baseUrl: string): Promise<void>; handle(m: ServerMessage): void; on(ev: "ready" | "layout" | "state", cb: () => void): () => void; dispose(): void }`(scene.json은 TwinData가 한 번 받은 것을 넘김. 준비 전 메시지 큐는 여기 한 곳)
  - `class TwinRuntime { constructor(d: RuntimeDeps); ready(): boolean; load(): Promise<void>; port(): TwinPort; dispose(): void }`, `interface RuntimeDeps { engine: TwinEngine; scene: TwinScene; data: { scene: SceneJson; index: IndexStore; base: string }; bus: EventBus | null; now(): number }`. 뒤 작업이 카메라(10)·절개(11)·고르기·select(22)·수준(23)·강조(24)·inset(25) 규칙을 메서드로 더하고 `runtime.test.ts`에 가짜 엔진·장면으로 시험한다.
  - `interface TwinPort { ready(): boolean; anchor(id: string): Vec3 | null; hidden(id: string): boolean; viewProj(): ArrayLike<number> | null; canvasRect(): { left: number; top: number; width: number; height: number } | null; onFrame(cb: () => void): () => void }`(뜻은 「공통 계약」)
  - `window.__twin3d`(`?debug=1`): Task 2의 모양. 이 작업에서는 `view("overview")`·`idle`·`mask`·`overlays`, `info()`의 `calls·triangles·textures·geometries·contexts·dpr·loadMs·gpu`(`WEBGL_debug_renderer_info` 이름). 나머지 spec·값은 Task 10·11·17·19·24에서.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { planFrame } from "../../src/twin3d/render/frame-plan";

const f = (over: Partial<Parameters<typeof planFrame>[0]> = {}) => planFrame({ moving: false, transitioning: false, spinning: false, animating: false, dirty: new Set(), ...over });

describe("바뀔 때만 그린다", () => {
  it("아무 일 없으면 안 그림", () => expect(f()).toEqual({ render: false, base: false, shadow: false, continuous: false }));
  it("카메라만 바뀌면 그림자는 다시 안 그림", () => expect(f({ dirty: new Set(["camera"]) })).toEqual({ render: true, base: true, shadow: false, continuous: false }));
  it("상태·배치·벽·불러오기·프리셋은 그림자도", () => {
    for (const r of ["state", "layout", "walls", "load", "preset"] as const) expect(f({ dirty: new Set([r]) }).shadow).toBe(true);
  });
  it("움직이는 동안은 계속", () => expect(f({ moving: true }).continuous).toBe(true));
  it("드럼 회전 중에도 계속, 본 장면도", () => expect(f({ spinning: true })).toMatchObject({ render: true, base: true }));
  it("오버레이만 바뀌거나 오버레이 움직임만 있으면 본 장면은 다시 안 그림", () => {
    expect(f({ dirty: new Set(["overlay"]) })).toEqual({ render: true, base: false, shadow: false, continuous: false });
    expect(f({ animating: true })).toMatchObject({ render: true, base: false, continuous: true });
    expect(f({ animating: true, dirty: new Set(["camera"]) }).base).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`ui/tests/twin3d/engine.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { TwinEngine } from "../../src/twin3d/render/TwinEngine";

function setup(animFps: number | null = null) {
  const queue: ((t: number) => void)[] = [];
  const info = { autoReset: false, reset: vi.fn(() => { info.render.calls = 0; info.render.triangles = 0; }), render: { calls: 0, triangles: 0 }, memory: { textures: 0, geometries: 0 } };
  const renderer = { setPixelRatio: vi.fn(), setSize: vi.fn(), dispose: vi.fn(), forceContextLoss: vi.fn(), shadowMap: { needsUpdate: false }, info };
  const shadows: boolean[] = [];
  const bases: boolean[] = [];
  // 패스 셋이 각자 render()를 부르는 합성기 흉내: 한 프레임에 calls 10씩 세 번
  const composer = { setPixelRatio: vi.fn(), setSize: vi.fn(), dispose: vi.fn(),
    render: vi.fn((_dt: number, o: { base: boolean }) => { shadows.push(renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate); bases.push(o.base); for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) info.render.calls += 10; }) };
  const caf = vi.fn();
  const engine = new TwinEngine(document.createElement("canvas"), {
    createRenderer: () => renderer, createComposer: () => composer,
    raf: (cb) => queue.push(cb), caf,
  }, { dprMax: 2, frustumHeight: 20, animFps });
  let clock = 0;
  const flush = (step = 16) => { clock += step; const run = queue.splice(0); run.forEach((cb) => cb(clock)); return run.length; };
  return { engine, renderer, composer, flush, queue, shadows, bases, caf };
}

describe("TwinEngine", () => {
  it("리사이즈: 캔버스·DPR·합성기·정사영 화면비를 한 번에", () => {
    const { engine, renderer, composer } = setup();
    engine.resize(1000, 500, 3);
    expect(renderer.setSize).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1000, 500, false);
    expect(renderer.setPixelRatio).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2);
    expect(composer.setPixelRatio).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2);
    expect(composer.setSize).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1000, 500);
    expect([engine.camera.left, engine.camera.right, engine.camera.top, engine.camera.bottom]).toEqual([-20, 20, 10, -10]);
  });
  it("한 번 그리고 멈춘다", () => {
    const { engine, flush, composer } = setup();
    engine.invalidate("camera");
    expect(flush()).toBe(1);
    expect(flush()).toBe(0);
    expect(composer.render).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
  it("상태가 바뀐 프레임에만 그림자 다시 그리기", () => {
    const { engine, flush, shadows } = setup();
    engine.invalidate("camera"); flush();
    engine.invalidate("state"); flush();
    expect(shadows).toEqual([false, true]);
  });
  it("움직이는 동안 계속 그리고, 멈추면 한 장 더 그린 뒤 쉰다", () => {
    const { engine, flush } = setup();
    engine.setActivity("moving", true);
    expect(flush()).toBe(1); expect(flush()).toBe(1);
    engine.setActivity("moving", false);
    expect(flush()).toBe(1); expect(flush()).toBe(0);
  });
  it("드로콜은 한 프레임 모든 패스의 합, 프레임마다 처음부터", () => {
    const { engine, renderer, flush } = setup();
    engine.invalidate("camera"); flush();
    expect(engine.lastFrame().calls).toBe(30);
    engine.invalidate("camera"); flush();
    expect(engine.lastFrame().calls).toBe(30);
    expect(renderer.info.reset).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });
  it("오버레이만 바뀐 프레임은 본 장면을 다시 그리지 않는다", () => {
    const { engine, flush, bases } = setup();
    engine.invalidate("camera"); flush();
    engine.invalidate("overlay"); flush();
    expect(bases).toEqual([true, false]);
  });
  it("오버레이 움직임만 있을 때는 animFps로 거른다(30이면 16ms 프레임 둘에 한 번)", () => {
    const { engine, flush, composer } = setup(30);
    engine.setActivity("animating", true);
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) flush(16);
    expect(composer.render).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
  });
  it("지우면 모두 dispose·문맥 놓기, 그 뒤에는 그리지 않음", () => {
    const { engine, renderer, composer, flush, caf } = setup();
    const before = TwinEngine.live;
    engine.invalidate("camera");
    engine.dispose();
    expect(composer.dispose).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(renderer.forceContextLoss).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(renderer.dispose).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(caf).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(TwinEngine.live).toBe(before - 1);
    engine.invalidate("camera");
    flush();
    expect(composer.render).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
  it("합성기를 바꾸면 옛 합성기를 패스까지 지운다", () => {
    const { engine, composer } = setup();
    const next = { setPixelRatio: vi.fn(), setSize: vi.fn(), render: vi.fn(), dispose: vi.fn() };
    engine.setComposer(next);
    expect(composer.dispose).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

`ui/tests/twin3d/build.test.ts`
```ts
import { BoxGeometry, Group, Mesh, MeshStandardMaterial, type Object3D } from "three";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { SceneJson } from "../../../src/contract/scene";
import { buildScene } from "../../src/twin3d/scene/build";

function asset(name: string): Object3D {
  const root = new Group(); root.name = name;
  const body = new Mesh(new BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1), new MeshStandardMaterial()); body.position.y = 0.5; root.add(body);
  const glow = new Mesh(new BoxGeometry(5, 5, 5), new MeshStandardMaterial()); glow.userData.effect = true; root.add(glow);
  const wrap = new Group(); wrap.add(root); return wrap;
}
const obj = (id: string, type: string, asset: string | null, x: number) => ({ id, name: id, type, asset, category: "furniture", mount: "floor", room: "A", plan: { position: [x, 0], facing_deg: 90, elevation: 0 }, position: [x, 0, 0], rotation: [0, 0, 0], scale: [1, 1, 1], interactive: true, attrs: {}, states: {}, state: {} });
const scene = { version: 1, units: "m", up: "+Y", front: "+Z", stage: {}, plan: { rooms: [], walls: [], doors: [], windows: [], ceiling_height: 2.3, wall_cut_height: 1.05 },
  objects: [obj("c1", "armchair", "assets/furniture/armchair.glb", 1), obj("c2", "armchair", "assets/furniture/armchair.glb", 3), obj("x", "mystery", null, 5)] } as unknown as SceneJson;

describe("buildScene", () => {
  it("종류마다 한 번 받고 복제, 위치 적용, 형상 공유, 모델 없으면 임시 상자", async () => {
    const urls: string[] = [];
    const built = await buildScene(scene, { load: async (u) => { urls.push(u); return asset("armchair"); } }, "/twin/");
    expect(urls).toEqual(["/twin/assets/furniture/armchair.glb"]);
    const c1 = built.registry.node("c1")!, c2 = built.registry.node("c2")!;
    expect(c2.position.x).toBe(3);
    expect((c1.getObjectByProperty("type", "Mesh") as Mesh).geometry).toBe((c2.getObjectByProperty("type", "Mesh") as Mesh).geometry);
    expect(built.placeholders).toEqual(["x"]);
    expect(built.registry.node("x")!.userData.placeholder).toBe(true);
  });
  it("고체 상자는 효과 부품을 빼고 잰다, 메시 → 물건 id", async () => {
    const built = await buildScene(scene, { load: async () => asset("armchair") }, "/twin/");
    const box = built.registry.solidBox("c1")!;
    expect(box.max.y).toBeCloseTo(1);
    const mesh = built.registry.node("c1")!.getObjectByProperty("type", "Mesh")!;
    expect(built.registry.objectOf(mesh)).toBe("c1");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { IndexStore } from "../../src/twin3d/core/index-store";
import { TwinScene } from "../../src/twin3d/scene/TwinScene";

describe("TwinScene", () => {
  it("준비 전 메시지는 쌓았다가 준비되면 받은 순서대로", async () => {
    const engine = { scene: { add: vi.fn(), remove: vi.fn() }, invalidate: vi.fn(), onBeforeRender: () => () => {}, setActivity: vi.fn() } as never;
    const scene = { version: 1, plan: { rooms: [], walls: [], doors: [], windows: [], ceiling_height: 2.3, wall_cut_height: 1.05 }, stage: {}, objects: [] } as never;
    const ts = new TwinScene(engine, { load: async () => { throw new Error("없음"); } }, new IndexStore(scene));
    const seen: string[] = [];
    ts.on("layout", () => seen.push("layout"));
    ts.on("state", () => seen.push("state"));
    ts.handle({ type: "state", state: { id: "c1", online: true, twin: {}, matter: {}, updatedAt: "t" } } as never);
    ts.handle({ type: "layout", op: "remove", id: "c1" } as never);
    expect(seen).toEqual([]);
    await ts.load(scene, "/twin/");
    expect(seen).toEqual(["state", "layout"]);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render } from "@testing-library/react";
import { StrictMode } from "react";

    const view = render(<TwinCanvas createEngine={create} onEngine={() => {}} />);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) view.rerender(<TwinCanvas createEngine={() => ({ resize: vi.fn(), dispose: vi.fn() }) as never} onEngine={() => {}} />);
```

`ui/tests/event-bus.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { EventBus } from "../src/lib/event-bus";

const hello = (boot = "b1") => ({ type: "hello", devices: 2, provider: null, hub: "memory", home: "mokryeon_180b", boot }) as never;
const state = (id: string, on: boolean) => ({ type: "state", state: { id, online: true, twin: { power: on }, matter: {}, updatedAt: "t" } }) as never;
const layout = (id: string) => ({ type: "layout", op: "remove", id }) as never;

describe("EventBus", () => {
  it("늦게 붙은 구독자도 접속 스냅숏을 받는다(기기별 마지막 state, layout 차례대로)", () => {
    const bus = new EventBus();
    [hello(), state("a", false), state("a", true), state("b", true), layout("x"), layout("y")].forEach((m) => bus.push(m));
    const got: [string, boolean][] = [];
    bus.subscribe((m, replay) => got.push([`${m.type}:${(m as { id?: string; state?: { id: string } }).state?.id ?? (m as { id?: string }).id ?? ""}`, replay]));
    expect(got).toEqual([["hello:", true], ["state:a", true], ["state:b", true], ["layout:x", true], ["layout:y", true]]);
    bus.push(state("b", false));
    expect(got.at(-1)).toEqual(["state:b", false]);
  });
  it("다시 접속(hello)하면 옛 state·layout은 버린다", () => {
    const bus = new EventBus();
    [hello(), layout("x"), hello(), layout("z")].forEach((m) => bus.push(m));
    const types: string[] = [];
    bus.subscribe((m) => types.push(m.type === "layout" ? `layout:${(m as { id: string }).id}` : m.type));
    expect(types).toEqual(["hello", "layout:z"]);
  });
});
```

`ui/tests/twin3d/twin-data.test.tsx`: 가짜 `fetch`로 `TwinDataProvider`를 그린다 → `scene.json`을 한 번만 받고, 버스에 `layout` add를 넣으면 `useTwinData().index.get().objects`에 새 id가 생긴다. `/twin/scene.json`이 404면 context 값은 null이고 오류를 던지지 않는다.

`ui/tests/twin3d/runtime.test.ts`: 가짜 엔진(`invalidate`·`onAfterRender`·`setActivity` 기록)·가짜 장면(`load`·`handle` 기록)·`EventBus`로 `TwinRuntime`을 만든다. R1: `load()` 전에 버스로 온 `state`가 장면이 준비된 뒤 `handle`로 한 번씩 간다(스냅숏 재생 포함). R2: `dispose()` 뒤 버스 메시지가 장면에 가지 않고 엔진 `dispose`가 한 번.

`ui/tests/twin3d/boundaries.test.ts`
```ts
// 의존 방향: 엔진·판단 모듈(.ts)은 React와 twin3d/ui를 모르고, core는 다른 twin3d 폴더를 모른다.
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, relative } from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const ROOT = join(__dirname, "../../src/twin3d");
const files = (dir: string): string[] => readdirSync(dir).flatMap((n) => { const p = join(dir, n); return statSync(p).isDirectory() ? files(p) : [p]; });
const imports = (p: string) => [...readFileSync(p, "utf8").matchAll(/from\s+["']([^"']+)["']/g)].map((m) => m[1]!);
const PURE = ["core", "scene", "camera", "zoom", "agent", "viz", "render", "minimap"];

describe("twin3d 의존 방향", () => {
  it("엔진·판단 .ts는 react·twin3d/ui를 import하지 않는다", () => {
    const bad = PURE.flatMap((d) => files(join(ROOT, d)).filter((p) => p.endsWith(".ts")).flatMap((p) =>
      imports(p).filter((s) => s === "react" || s.startsWith("react/") || /(^|\/)ui(\/|$)/.test(s)).map((s) => `${relative(ROOT, p)} → ${s}`)));
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("core는 다른 twin3d 폴더를 import하지 않는다", () => {
    const bad = files(join(ROOT, "core")).flatMap((p) => imports(p).filter((s) => /^\.\.\/(scene|camera|zoom|agent|viz|render|minimap|ui)\b/.test(s)).map((s) => `${relative(ROOT, p)} → ${s}`));
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```
(`../../../../src/contract/…`는 twin3d 밖이라 허용. `twin3d/ui`는 `ui/` 이름을 뜻하고, `ui/src/` 같은 바깥 경로는 쓰지 않는다.)

`ui/src/twin3d/render/frame-plan.ts`
```ts
// 프레임 계획: 바뀐 것이 있을 때만 그리고, 그림자는 장면이 바뀐 프레임에만 다시 그린다. 멈추면 GPU 0.
// 오버레이(에이전트 띠·눈·고리)만 바뀌면 본 장면(RenderPass·GTAO·아웃라인) 결과를 다시 쓰고 오버레이부터 다시 그린다.
export type DirtyReason = "load" | "resize" | "walls" | "state" | "layout" | "preset" | "selection" | "overlay" | "camera" | "highlight";
export interface FrameFlags { moving: boolean; transitioning: boolean; spinning: boolean; animating: boolean; dirty: ReadonlySet<DirtyReason> }
const SHADOW: DirtyReason[] = ["load", "walls", "state", "layout", "preset"];

export function planFrame(f: FrameFlags): { render: boolean; base: boolean; shadow: boolean; continuous: boolean } {
  const continuous = f.moving || f.transitioning || f.spinning || f.animating;
  const render = continuous || f.dirty.size > 0;
  const base = f.moving || f.transitioning || f.spinning || [...f.dirty].some((r) => r !== "overlay");
  return { continuous, render, base: render && base, shadow: SHADOW.some((r) => f.dirty.has(r)) };
}
```

`ui/src/twin3d/render/TwinEngine.ts`
```ts
// 그리는 엔진: 렌더러·장면·정사영 카메라·바뀔 때만 도는 루프·리사이즈·정리. React 상태를 읽지 않는다.
import { OrthographicCamera, Scene } from "three";
import { planFrame, type DirtyReason } from "./frame-plan";

export interface RendererLike {
  setPixelRatio(r: number): void; setSize(w: number, h: number, style: boolean): void; dispose(): void; forceContextLoss(): void;
  shadowMap: { needsUpdate: boolean };
  info: { autoReset: boolean; reset(): void; render: { calls: number; triangles: number }; memory: { textures: number; geometries: number } };
}
export interface ComposerLike { setPixelRatio(r: number): void; setSize(w: number, h: number): void; render(dt: number, opts: { base: boolean }): void; dispose(): void }
export interface EngineDeps { createRenderer(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement): RendererLike; createComposer(r: RendererLike, e: TwinEngine): ComposerLike; raf(cb: (t: number) => void): number; caf(id: number): void }
type Activity = "moving" | "transitioning" | "spinning" | "animating";

export class TwinEngine {
  /** 살아 있는 엔진 수(= WebGL 문맥 수). 붙였다 떼기 검사에 쓴다. */
  static live = 0;
  readonly scene = new Scene();
  readonly overlay = new Scene();
  readonly camera = new OrthographicCamera(-1, 1, 1, -1, 1, 100);
  readonly renderer: RendererLike;
  frames = 0;
  private composer: ComposerLike;
  private dirty = new Set<DirtyReason>();
  private active = new Set<Activity>();
  private frame: number | null = null;
  private last = 0;
  private lastRender = -Infinity;
  private stats = { calls: 0, triangles: 0 };
  private size = { width: 1, height: 1, dpr: 1 };
  private before = new Set<(dt: number) => void>();
  private after = new Set<() => void>();
  private disposed = false;

  constructor(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, private deps: EngineDeps, private opts: { dprMax: number; frustumHeight: number; animFps?: number | null }) {
    this.renderer = deps.createRenderer(canvas);
    this.renderer.info.autoReset = false;
    this.composer = deps.createComposer(this.renderer, this);
    TwinEngine.live++;
  }

  viewport() { return { ...this.size }; }
  lastFrame() { return { ...this.stats }; }

  resize(width: number, height: number, dpr: number) {
    const ratio = Math.min(dpr, this.opts.dprMax);
    const fh = this.opts.frustumHeight;
    this.size = { width, height, dpr: ratio };
    this.renderer.setPixelRatio(ratio);
    this.renderer.setSize(width, height, false);
    this.composer.setPixelRatio(ratio);
    this.composer.setSize(width, height);
    const aspect = width / Math.max(1, height);
    Object.assign(this.camera, { left: (-fh * aspect) / 2, right: (fh * aspect) / 2, top: fh / 2, bottom: -fh / 2 });
    this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    this.invalidate("resize");
  }

  setComposer(c: ComposerLike) { this.composer.dispose(); this.composer = c; this.composer.setPixelRatio(this.size.dpr); this.composer.setSize(this.size.width, this.size.height); this.invalidate("preset"); }

  invalidate(reason: DirtyReason) { if (this.disposed) return; this.dirty.add(reason); this.schedule(); }

  setActivity(k: Activity, on: boolean) {
    if (this.disposed) return;
    if (on) this.active.add(k); else if (this.active.delete(k)) this.dirty.add("camera");
    this.schedule();
  }

  onBeforeRender(cb: (dt: number) => void) { this.before.add(cb); return () => { this.before.delete(cb); }; }
  onAfterRender(cb: () => void) { this.after.add(cb); return () => { this.after.delete(cb); }; }

  private schedule() { if (this.frame === null && !this.disposed) this.frame = this.deps.raf((t) => this.tick(t)); }

  private tick(t: number) {
    if (this.disposed) return;
    this.frame = null;
    const flags = { moving: this.active.has("moving"), transitioning: this.active.has("transitioning"), spinning: this.active.has("spinning"), animating: this.active.has("animating"), dirty: this.dirty };
    const animOnly = (flags.spinning || flags.animating) && !flags.moving && !flags.transitioning && this.dirty.size === 0;
    if (animOnly && this.opts.animFps && t - this.lastRender < 1000 / this.opts.animFps - 2) { this.schedule(); return; }
    const dt = this.last ? Math.min(0.1, (t - this.last) / 1000) : 0;
    this.last = t;
    this.before.forEach((cb) => cb(dt));
    const plan = planFrame(flags);
    if (plan.shadow) this.renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = true;
    if (plan.render) {
      this.renderer.info.reset();
      this.composer.render(dt, { base: plan.base });
      this.stats = { calls: this.renderer.info.render.calls, triangles: this.renderer.info.render.triangles };
      this.frames++; this.lastRender = t;
      this.after.forEach((cb) => cb());
    }
    this.renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = false;
    this.dirty.clear();
    if (plan.continuous) this.schedule(); else this.last = 0;
  }

  dispose() {
    if (this.disposed) return;
    this.disposed = true;
    if (this.frame !== null) this.deps.caf(this.frame);
    this.frame = null;
    this.composer.dispose();
    this.renderer.forceContextLoss();
    this.renderer.dispose();
    this.before.clear(); this.after.clear();
    TwinEngine.live--;
  }
}
```
(시험 「상태가 바뀐 프레임에만 그림자」는 합성기 `render` 안에서 `needsUpdate`를 읽는다. 위 순서가 그 순서다. 「드럼 회전」은 본 장면이 움직이므로 animFps로 거르되 base는 참이다.)

`create-engine.ts`(브라우저 전용, 시험 없음):
```ts
// 실제 WebGLRenderer와 기본 합성기(RenderPass → 오버레이 → OutputPass). 품질 패스·본 장면 보관은 Task 16·18에서 composer.ts로 바꿔 낀다.
import { NeutralToneMapping, PCFShadowMap, SRGBColorSpace, WebGLRenderer } from "three";
import { EffectComposer } from "three/examples/jsm/postprocessing/EffectComposer.js";
import { OutputPass } from "three/examples/jsm/postprocessing/OutputPass.js";
import type { Pass } from "three/examples/jsm/postprocessing/Pass.js";
import { RenderPass } from "three/examples/jsm/postprocessing/RenderPass.js";
import { FRUSTUM_HEIGHT } from "../camera/fit";
import { TwinEngine, type ComposerLike } from "./TwinEngine";

/** EffectComposer.dispose()는 r187에서 자기 렌더 타깃·copyPass만 지운다. 넣은 패스도 모두 지우게 감싼다. */
export function wrapComposer(c: EffectComposer, passes: Pass[]): ComposerLike {
  return {
    setPixelRatio: (r) => c.setPixelRatio(r), setSize: (w, h) => c.setSize(w, h),
    render: (dt) => c.render(dt),
    dispose: () => { passes.forEach((p) => p.dispose()); c.dispose(); },
  };
}

export function createEngine(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, dprMax = 2): TwinEngine {
  return new TwinEngine(canvas, {
    createRenderer: (c) => {
      const r = new WebGLRenderer({ canvas: c, antialias: false, powerPreference: "high-performance", stencil: false });
      r.outputColorSpace = SRGBColorSpace;
      r.toneMapping = NeutralToneMapping; // 결정 D7. Task 16에서 ?tone=으로 바꿔 볼 수 있게 함
      r.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
      r.shadowMap.enabled = true;
      r.shadowMap.type = PCFShadowMap;
      r.shadowMap.autoUpdate = false;
      r.localClippingEnabled = true;
      r.info.autoReset = false;
      return r as never;
    },
    createComposer: (r, e) => {
      const c = new EffectComposer(r as unknown as WebGLRenderer);
      const over = new RenderPass(e.overlay, e.camera); over.clear = false; over.clearDepth = true;
      const passes: Pass[] = [new RenderPass(e.scene, e.camera), over, new OutputPass()];
      passes.forEach((p) => c.addPass(p));
      return wrapComposer(c, passes);
    },
    raf: (cb) => requestAnimationFrame(cb), caf: (id) => cancelAnimationFrame(id),
  }, { dprMax, frustumHeight: FRUSTUM_HEIGHT });
}
```

`scene/registry.ts`: 노드 등록부. `add`는 노드와 모든 자식에 `WeakMap<Object3D, id>`를 걸고, `solidBox`는 `userData.effect`·`userData.no_bounds`가 있는 자식을 빼고 `Box3.expandByObject`로 재서 캐시(이동·삭제 때 비움). `walls`·`floors` 구조 노드는 `indexStructure(root)`로 `wall_<id>`의 `userData.wall`(JSON `{id, kind, faces}`)과 자식 `_lower`·`_upper`(Task 4의 `span`·`rooms`, `findWallParts` 배열)를 읽어 `wallPieces()`를 만들고(구간마다 한 조각, `kind`·`faces`는 holder 것, 나뉘지 않은 벽의 `rooms`는 `faces`의 방), `floor_<room>` 메시를 방 id에 잇는다.
`build.test.ts`에 더한다: 가짜 구조 트리(나뉜 벽 `wall_E10` = holder `userData.wall = '{"id":"E10","kind":"interior","faces":[{"room":"A","normal":[1,0]},{"room":"B","normal":[-1,0]}]}'` + 자식 `wall_E10_0_lower/upper`(rooms `["A"]`)·`wall_E10_1_lower/upper`(rooms `["B"]`), 나뉘지 않은 벽 `wall_E01` + `wall_E01_lower/upper`, 바닥 `floor_A`) → `wallPieces()`가 `E10#0`·`E10#1`·`E01` 세 조각(`kind`·`faces`·`rooms`·lower·upper 각 1개), `wallOf(wall_E10_1_upper)` = `{ wall: "E10", span: 1, rooms: ["B"], part: "upper" }`, `roomOfFloor(floor_A)` = `"A"`.

`scene/build.ts`: `uniqueAssets(objects)`로 종류마다 한 번 `src.load(baseUrl + asset)` → `assetRoot(gltf, asset)`. 물건마다 `template.clone(true)`(형상·재질 공유), `name = id`, `applyTransform(node, entry)`, `userData = { id, room, category, mount, provenance }`. 모델이 없거나 받기 실패면 `boxSpec(entry)` 크기 상자 + `userData.placeholder = true` + 재질 `#B8BCC2`(거칠기 0.9). 받은 뒤 `prepare`: 투명 재질은 `castShadow=false`, 투명 양면은 `forceSinglePass=true`.

`scene/loaders.ts`: `GLTFLoader` + `setMeshoptDecoder(MeshoptDecoder)` + `setKTX2Loader(new KTX2Loader().setTranscoderPath("/twin3d/basis/").detectSupport(renderer))`, URL별 `Promise` 캐시.

`scene/TwinScene.ts`: `load(scene, base)` → `buildScene` → `engine.scene.add(root)` → `renderer.compileAsync`(있으면) → `ready = true` → 쌓인 메시지를 받은 순서대로 `apply`. `handle(m)`: 준비 전이면 쌓음(큐는 여기 하나). `state` → `createLiveSync().handleMessage`(어댑터 `getAttrs/getState/setState(id, attr, value)` → `applyState`, 빛 콜백은 Task 12 전까지 아무것도 안 함) → `invalidate("state")`. `layout` → `routeLayout` → move/add/remove(빛 풀 반납 포함) → `invalidate("layout")`. 색인은 TwinData가 `IndexStore.apply`로 바꾸고 TwinScene은 `index.get()`만 읽는다. 세탁기·건조기 드럼 회전은 `tickSpins`를 `onBeforeRender`에서 부르고 돌고 있으면 `setActivity("spinning", true)`.

`ui/TwinCanvas.tsx`
```tsx
// <canvas> 하나와 엔진 수명. 엔진은 마운트 때 한 번만 만든다(부모가 다시 그려도 그대로). 엔진 만들기는 바꿔 끼울 수 있다(시험).
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import type { TwinEngine } from "../render/TwinEngine";
import { createEngine as realCreate } from "../render/create-engine";

type EngineLike = Pick<TwinEngine, "resize" | "dispose">;

export function TwinCanvas({ createEngine = realCreate as (c: HTMLCanvasElement) => EngineLike, onEngine, children }: {
  createEngine?: (c: HTMLCanvasElement) => EngineLike; onEngine?: (e: EngineLike | null) => void; children?: ReactNode;
}) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const create = useRef(createEngine);
  const notify = useRef(onEngine);
  notify.current = onEngine;
  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = ref.current!;
    const engine = create.current(canvas);
    const fit = () => { const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); engine.resize(Math.max(1, r.width), Math.max(1, r.height), window.devicePixelRatio || 1); };
    const ro = typeof ResizeObserver === "function" ? new ResizeObserver(fit) : null;
    ro?.observe(canvas);
    const mq = window.matchMedia(`(resolution: ${window.devicePixelRatio || 1}dppx)`);
    mq.addEventListener("change", fit);
    fit();
    notify.current?.(engine);
    return () => { ro?.disconnect(); mq.removeEventListener("change", fit); notify.current?.(null); engine.dispose(); };
  }, []);
  return (
    <div className="absolute inset-0">
      <canvas ref={ref} className="block h-full w-full touch-none" />
      <div data-twin-overlay className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 [&>*]:pointer-events-auto">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`lib/event-bus.ts`: 위 Interfaces. `useEventBus()`는 `useRef`로 버스를 한 번 만들고 `useEvents(useCallback((m) => bus.push(m), []), useCallback((open) => bus.connection(open), []))`.
`ui/TwinData.tsx`: 위 Interfaces. `/api/health`의 `twin`이 거짓이면 받지 않는다.
`TwinRuntime.ts`: 엔진·장면·버스 구독을 묶는다. 버스 구독은 장면을 만든 뒤 붙고 스냅숏을 받아 `scene.handle`로 넘긴다(준비 전이면 TwinScene 큐). `port()`는 엔진·등록부로 `TwinPort`를 만든다(`canvasRect`는 덮개 층 기준, `hidden`은 늘 false).
`Twin3d.tsx`: `memo`. `useTwinData()`·`EventBusContext`에서 데이터와 버스를 읽고, `TwinCanvas`의 `onEngine`에서 `TwinRuntime`을 한 번 만들고 지운다. 메시지를 prop으로 받지 않는다(Dashboard가 기기 상태로 다시 그려도 엔진은 그대로). 데이터가 null이면(3D 없음) 지금 iframe 경로와 같은 안내.
`port.ts`의 `TwinPort`를 context로 내린다. `iframe-port.ts`는 지금 `ui/src/twin/viewer.ts`·`anchors.ts`·`visibility.ts`를 감싸 같은 `TwinPort`를 낸다(`canvasRect`는 iframe 상자를 덮개 층 기준으로 바꿈, `hidden`은 `hiddenByFocus`).
`debug.ts`: `?debug=1`일 때만 `window.__twin3d`(Task 2 모양). 이때 대시보드 칸(지금 화면의 상태 알약·눈 창·판단 기록, Task 25부터 마스코트 칸·폰 칸·트윈 진행도·칩)은 그리지 않아 캔버스 전체가 쓸 수 있는 화면이 된다(캡처 통계가 패널에 흔들리지 않게). `?debug=panel`은 손잡이 + 측정 패널(Task 19)이고 대시보드 패널도 그대로 보인다. `idle()`은 다음 프레임 뒤 `active`가 비어 있으면 끝나는 Promise. `mask()`는 층 0 장면을 흰 `MeshBasicMaterial` 덮어쓰기·검정 배경·톤매핑 없이 `renderer.render`로 한 장 그려 같은 작업 안에서 `readPixels`로 읽고(합성기 거치지 않음) 다음 프레임을 다시 그리게 한다. `overlays(show)`는 `[data-twin-overlay]`의 `visibility`를 바꾸고 다음 프레임을 기다린다. `info()`는 `engine.lastFrame()`(모든 패스 합)·`renderer.info.memory`·`TwinEngine.live`·불러오기 시간.
`Dashboard.tsx`: `const bus = useEventBus()`로 소켓 하나. 지금 쓰는 메시지 처리는 `bus.subscribe`로 옮긴다. 지금 `onMessage`는 `homeId`(집 바뀜 새로 고침)에 따라 다시 만들어지므로 처리기는 ref로 들고 구독은 마운트 때 한 번만 붙인다(다시 구독하면 스냅숏을 또 받는다). 연결 상태 `setConnected`는 `subscribe`의 `onOpen`으로 넘긴다. `<EventBusContext.Provider value={bus}><TwinDataProvider bus={bus}>…`로 감싼다. `new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("twin") === "three"`이면 `<Twin3d kind="dashboard">`(덮개 자식은 같음), 아니면 지금 `<TwinStage>`.
`CLAUDE.md` 규칙 표에 한 줄: `| twin3d 엔진·판단 모듈은 React·twin3d/ui를 모르고 core는 다른 폴더를 모른다. | ui/tests/twin3d/boundaries.test.ts |`

### Task 10: 카메라 컨트롤러(정사영·회전 제한·커서 확대·전체 맞춤)

> 빛 굽기로 바뀜. 시작하기 전에 「빛 굽기로 바꾸는 것」 표대로 이 본문을 고친다.

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/twin3d/camera/CameraController.ts`, `ui/src/twin3d/camera/create-controls.ts`, `ui/tests/twin3d/camera-controller.test.ts`, `ui/tests/twin3d/controls-wiring.test.ts`
- Modify: `ui/src/twin3d/TwinRuntime.ts`(컨트롤러 만들기, 불러온 뒤 전체 맞춤, 리사이즈 때 다시 맞춤), `ui/src/twin3d/debug.ts`(`view("room:…" | "object:…" | "angle:…", { instant })`, `info()`의 `zoom·viewHeight·targetNdc·usable·objects`), `ui/tests/twin3d/runtime.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `fitView`, `viewBasis`, `FIT`, `FRUSTUM_HEIGHT`, `housePoints/roomPoints/objectPoints`(Task 8), `HomeIndex`, `TwinEngine.setActivity`
- Produces:
  - `type ControlsEvent = "controlstart" | "control" | "controlend" | "transitionstart" | "update" | "wake" | "rest" | "sleep"`(camera-controls 3.1.2 이벤트 이름)
  - `interface ControlsLike { setLookAt(px: number, py: number, pz: number, tx: number, ty: number, tz: number, smooth: boolean): Promise<void>; zoomTo(z: number, smooth: boolean): Promise<void>; setFocalOffset(x: number, y: number, z: number, smooth: boolean): Promise<void>; rotateTo(azimuth: number, polar: number, smooth: boolean): Promise<void>; update(dt: number): boolean; stop(): void; azimuthAngle: number; polarAngle: number; minPolarAngle: number; maxPolarAngle: number; minAzimuthAngle: number; maxAzimuthAngle: number; minZoom: number; maxZoom: number; addEventListener(t: ControlsEvent, fn: () => void): void; removeEventListener(t: ControlsEvent, fn: () => void): void }`
  - `wireControls(engine: Pick<TwinEngine, "setActivity" | "invalidate" | "onBeforeRender">, controls: ControlsLike, canvas: HTMLElement): () => void`: `controlstart`·`control`·`transitionstart` → `setActivity("moving", true)`, `sleep` → `setActivity("moving", false)`, 캔버스 `wheel` → `invalidate("camera")`, 매 프레임 `controls.update(dt)`가 참이면 `invalidate("camera")`(휠은 `controlstart`를 내지 않음)
  - `type CameraCommand = { kind: "overview" } | { kind: "room"; roomId: string } | { kind: "object"; objectId: string; include: Plan2[] } | { kind: "angle"; azimuthDeg: number; elevationDeg: number }`
  - `const CAMERA = { elevationDeg: 42, azimuthDeg: 45, minElevationDeg: 30, maxElevationDeg: 60, azimuthRangeDeg: 45, distance: 40, smoothTime: 0.35, draggingSmoothTime: 0.12, maxZoomViewHeight: 2 }`
  - `interface CameraDeps { controls: ControlsLike; index: HomeIndex; viewport(): Viewport; raised(roomId: string | null): { a: Plan2; b: Plan2 }[]; objectBox(id: string): { min: Vec3; max: Vec3 } | null; setTransitioning(on: boolean): void; onManual(kind: "drag" | "wheel" | "touch"): void }`
  - `class CameraController { constructor(d: CameraDeps); apply(cmd: CameraCommand, smooth?: boolean): Promise<void>; refit(): Promise<void>; current(): CameraCommand; attachInput(el: HTMLElement): () => void; heading(): Plan2; basis(): ViewBasis }`

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 시험**

`ui/tests/twin3d/camera-controller.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { CAMERA, CameraController } from "../../src/twin3d/camera/CameraController";
import { FIT, FRUSTUM_HEIGHT, fitView, viewBasis } from "../../src/twin3d/camera/fit";
import { housePoints } from "../../src/twin3d/camera/targets";
import { homeIndexFrom } from "../../src/twin3d/core/home-index";
import mokryeon from "../fixtures/mokryeon-scene.json";

const DEG = Math.PI / 180;
function fake() {
  const resolvers: (() => void)[] = [];
  const later = () => new Promise<void>((r) => resolvers.push(r));
  const c = {
    azimuthAngle: CAMERA.azimuthDeg * DEG, polarAngle: (90 - CAMERA.elevationDeg) * DEG,
    minPolarAngle: 0, maxPolarAngle: Math.PI, minAzimuthAngle: -Infinity, maxAzimuthAngle: Infinity, minZoom: 0.01, maxZoom: Infinity,
    setLookAt: vi.fn(later), zoomTo: vi.fn((_z: number) => { minAtZoom.push(c.minZoom); return later(); }), setFocalOffset: vi.fn(later), rotateTo: vi.fn(later),
    update: vi.fn(() => false), stop: vi.fn(), addEventListener: vi.fn(), removeEventListener: vi.fn(),
  };
  const minAtZoom: number[] = [];
  return { c, minAtZoom, finish: async () => { resolvers.splice(0).forEach((r) => r()); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0)); } };
}
const index = homeIndexFrom(mokryeon as never);

function make() {
  const { c, finish, minAtZoom } = fake();
  const transitioning: boolean[] = [];
  const manual: string[] = [];
  const vp = { width: 1280, height: 800, insets: { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 } };
  const cam = new CameraController({ controls: c, index, viewport: () => vp, raised: () => [], objectBox: () => ({ min: [1, 0, 1], max: [2, 1, 2] }), setTransitioning: (on) => transitioning.push(on), onManual: (k) => manual.push(k) });
  return { c, finish, cam, transitioning, manual, vp, minAtZoom };
}

describe("CameraController", () => {
  it("회전 제한: 고도 30~60°, 방위 45±45°", () => {
    const { c } = make();
    expect(c.minPolarAngle).toBeCloseTo(30 * DEG);
    expect(c.maxPolarAngle).toBeCloseTo(60 * DEG);
    expect(c.minAzimuthAngle).toBeCloseTo(0);
    expect(c.maxAzimuthAngle).toBeCloseTo(90 * DEG);
  });
  it("전체 보기 = fitView(집 점, 지금 방향) 그대로, 부드럽게", async () => {
    const { c, cam, vp } = make();
    void cam.apply({ kind: "overview" });
    const want = fitView(housePoints(index, []), viewBasis(45, 48), vp, FRUSTUM_HEIGHT, FIT.overview.margin, FIT.overview);
    const [, , , tx, ty, tz, smooth] = c.setLookAt.mock.calls[0]!;
    expect([tx, ty, tz].map((v) => Math.round(v * 1000))).toEqual(want.target.map((v) => Math.round(v * 1000)));
    expect(smooth).toBe(true);
    expect(c.zoomTo.mock.calls[0]![0]).toBeCloseTo(want.zoom);
  });
  it("전체 맞춘 뒤 더 멀리는 못 빼고(×0.9), 보기 높이 2m보다 가까이 못 감", async () => {
    const { c, cam, finish } = make();
    const p = cam.apply({ kind: "overview" }); await finish(); await p;
    expect(c.minZoom).toBeCloseTo((c.zoomTo.mock.calls[0]![0] as number) * 0.9);
    expect(c.maxZoom).toBeCloseTo(FRUSTUM_HEIGHT / 2);
  });
  it("창을 좁히고 다시 맞추면 하한을 먼저 낮춰 새 맞춤 값이 잘리지 않는다", async () => {
    const { c, cam, finish, vp, minAtZoom } = make();
    const p = cam.apply({ kind: "overview" }); await finish(); await p;
    vp.width = 640; // 정사영 zoom은 화면비에 따라 바뀐다(1.6:1 → 0.8:1)
    const q = cam.refit(); await finish(); await q;
    const z = c.zoomTo.mock.calls.at(-1)![0] as number;
    expect(z).toBeLessThan(c.zoomTo.mock.calls[0]![0] as number);
    expect(minAtZoom.at(-1)!).toBeLessThanOrEqual(z);
  });
  it("전환 중 새 명령이 오면 마지막 것이 지금 대상이고, 그것이 끝나야 전환 끝", async () => {
    const { cam, finish, transitioning } = make();
    void cam.apply({ kind: "room", roomId: "kitchen" });
    void cam.apply({ kind: "object", objectId: "fridge_bbc363a3", include: [] });
    expect(cam.current()).toEqual({ kind: "object", objectId: "fridge_bbc363a3", include: [] });
    await finish();
    expect(transitioning.at(-1)).toBe(false);
    expect(transitioning.filter((v) => v === false)).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it("끌기·휠·터치 시작은 수동 신호, 코드로 옮기는 것은 아님", () => {
    const { cam, manual } = make();
    const el = document.createElement("div");
    const off = cam.attachInput(el);
    el.dispatchEvent(new Event("pointerdown")); el.dispatchEvent(new Event("wheel")); el.dispatchEvent(new Event("touchstart"));
    void cam.apply({ kind: "overview" });
    expect(manual).toEqual(["drag", "wheel", "touch"]);
    off();
  });
  it("사용자가 손대면 진행 중 전환을 멈춘다", () => {
    const { c, cam } = make();
    const el = document.createElement("div");
    cam.attachInput(el);
    void cam.apply({ kind: "room", roomId: "kitchen" });
    el.dispatchEvent(new Event("pointerdown"));
    expect(c.stop).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

`ui/tests/twin3d/controls-wiring.test.ts`: 가짜 컨트롤(이벤트 등록부·`update`가 돌려줄 값)과 가짜 엔진으로 `wireControls`. `controlstart` → moving 켜짐, `control` → 켜짐 유지, `rest` → 그대로, `sleep` → 꺼짐, `transitionstart` → 켜짐, 캔버스 `wheel` → `invalidate("camera")`, `onBeforeRender` 콜백에서 `update`가 참이면 `invalidate("camera")`. 되돌림 함수를 부르면 리스너가 모두 빠짐.

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패 확인** — `npx vitest run --root ui tests/twin3d/camera-controller.test.ts` → FAIL
- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`ui/src/twin3d/camera/CameraController.ts`
```ts
// 카메라 컨트롤러: 명령(전체·방·물체·각도) → fitView → camera-controls 부드러운 이동. 판단 로직은 없다.
import type { Plan2, Vec3 } from "../../../../src/contract/events.ts";
import type { HomeIndex } from "../core/home-index";
import { FIT, FRUSTUM_HEIGHT, fitView, viewBasis, type ViewBasis, type Viewport } from "./fit";
import { housePoints, objectPoints, roomPoints } from "./targets";

const DEG = Math.PI / 180;
export const CAMERA = { elevationDeg: 42, azimuthDeg: 45, minElevationDeg: 30, maxElevationDeg: 60, azimuthRangeDeg: 45, distance: 40, smoothTime: 0.35, draggingSmoothTime: 0.12, maxZoomViewHeight: 2 } as const;

export interface ControlsLike {
  setLookAt(px: number, py: number, pz: number, tx: number, ty: number, tz: number, smooth: boolean): Promise<void>;
  zoomTo(z: number, smooth: boolean): Promise<void>;
  setFocalOffset(x: number, y: number, z: number, smooth: boolean): Promise<void>;
  rotateTo(azimuth: number, polar: number, smooth: boolean): Promise<void>;
  update(dt: number): boolean; stop(): void;
  azimuthAngle: number; polarAngle: number;
  minPolarAngle: number; maxPolarAngle: number; minAzimuthAngle: number; maxAzimuthAngle: number; minZoom: number; maxZoom: number;
  addEventListener(t: ControlsEvent, fn: () => void): void;
  removeEventListener(t: ControlsEvent, fn: () => void): void;
}
export type ControlsEvent = "controlstart" | "control" | "controlend" | "transitionstart" | "update" | "wake" | "rest" | "sleep";
export type CameraCommand =
  | { kind: "overview" } | { kind: "room"; roomId: string }
  | { kind: "object"; objectId: string; include: Plan2[] } | { kind: "angle"; azimuthDeg: number; elevationDeg: number };
export interface CameraDeps {
  controls: ControlsLike; index: HomeIndex; viewport(): Viewport;
  raised(roomId: string | null): { a: Plan2; b: Plan2 }[];
  objectBox(id: string): { min: Vec3; max: Vec3 } | null;
  setTransitioning(on: boolean): void; onManual(kind: "drag" | "wheel" | "touch"): void;
}

export class CameraController {
  private cmd: CameraCommand = { kind: "overview" };
  private seq = 0;

  constructor(private d: CameraDeps) {
    const c = d.controls;
    c.minPolarAngle = (90 - CAMERA.maxElevationDeg) * DEG;
    c.maxPolarAngle = (90 - CAMERA.minElevationDeg) * DEG;
    c.minAzimuthAngle = (CAMERA.azimuthDeg - CAMERA.azimuthRangeDeg) * DEG;
    c.maxAzimuthAngle = (CAMERA.azimuthDeg + CAMERA.azimuthRangeDeg) * DEG;
    c.maxZoom = FRUSTUM_HEIGHT / CAMERA.maxZoomViewHeight;
  }

  current() { return this.cmd; }
  basis(): ViewBasis { return viewBasis(this.d.controls.azimuthAngle / DEG, this.d.controls.polarAngle / DEG); }
  heading(): Plan2 { const b = this.basis().back; const l = Math.hypot(b[0], b[2]) || 1; return [b[0] / l, b[2] / l]; }

  async apply(cmd: CameraCommand, smooth = true): Promise<void> {
    this.cmd = cmd;
    const seq = ++this.seq;
    const c = this.d.controls;
    if (cmd.kind === "angle") {
      this.d.setTransitioning(true);
      await c.rotateTo(cmd.azimuthDeg * DEG, (90 - cmd.elevationDeg) * DEG, smooth);
      if (seq === this.seq) return this.apply({ kind: "overview" }, smooth);
      return;
    }
    const { points, fit } = this.pointsFor(cmd);
    const r = fitView(points, this.basis(), this.d.viewport(), FRUSTUM_HEIGHT, fit.margin, fit);
    const b = this.basis().back;
    const t = r.target;
    this.d.setTransitioning(true);
    // camera-controls zoomTo는 minZoom~maxZoom으로 자른다. 창이 작아져 새 전체 보기 zoom이 옛 하한보다 작으면 먼저 하한을 낮춘다.
    c.minZoom = Math.min(c.minZoom, r.zoom * 0.9);
    await Promise.all([
      c.setLookAt(t[0] + b[0] * CAMERA.distance, t[1] + b[1] * CAMERA.distance, t[2] + b[2] * CAMERA.distance, t[0], t[1], t[2], smooth),
      c.zoomTo(r.zoom, smooth),
      c.setFocalOffset(r.offset[0], r.offset[1], 0, smooth),
    ]);
    if (seq !== this.seq) return;
    if (cmd.kind === "overview") c.minZoom = r.zoom * 0.9;
    this.d.setTransitioning(false);
  }

  refit() { return this.apply(this.cmd, false); }

  attachInput(el: HTMLElement): () => void {
    const map: [string, "drag" | "wheel" | "touch"][] = [["pointerdown", "drag"], ["wheel", "wheel"], ["touchstart", "touch"]];
    const handlers = map.map(([ev, kind]) => {
      const h = () => { this.d.controls.stop(); this.seq++; this.d.setTransitioning(false); this.d.onManual(kind); };
      el.addEventListener(ev, h, { passive: true });
      return [ev, h] as const;
    });
    return () => handlers.forEach(([ev, h]) => el.removeEventListener(ev, h));
  }

  private pointsFor(cmd: Exclude<CameraCommand, { kind: "angle" }>) {
    const i = this.d.index;
    if (cmd.kind === "room") {
      const room = i.rooms.get(cmd.roomId);
      if (room) return { points: roomPoints(room, i.cut, i.ceiling, this.d.raised(room.id)), fit: FIT.room };
    }
    if (cmd.kind === "object") {
      const box = this.d.objectBox(cmd.objectId);
      if (box) return { points: objectPoints(box, cmd.include), fit: FIT.object };
    }
    return { points: housePoints(i, this.d.raised(null)), fit: FIT.overview };
  }
}
```
(사용자가 손대면 진행 중 전환을 `stop()`으로 멈추고 `seq`를 올려 끝 처리를 건너뛴다. 사용자 조작과 부딪치지 않는다.)

`create-controls.ts`: `CameraControls.install({ THREE: { Vector2, Vector3, Vector4, Quaternion, Matrix4, Spherical, Box3, Sphere, Raycaster } })`, `new CameraControls(engine.camera, canvas)`, `dollyToCursor = true`, `smoothTime = CAMERA.smoothTime`, `draggingSmoothTime = CAMERA.draggingSmoothTime`, 마우스 왼쪽 ROTATE·오른쪽 TRUCK·휠 ZOOM, 터치 한 손가락 ROTATE·두 손가락 TOUCH_ZOOM_TRUCK, `setBoundary(집 상자 + 1m)`, 그리고 `wireControls`(위 Interfaces).
`TwinRuntime`: 처음 각도 `rotateTo(45°, 48°)` 뒤 `apply({kind:"overview"}, false)`. 리사이즈 때 `refit()`. 쓸 수 있는 화면 `insets`는 덮개 패널(`[data-twin-inset]`) 사각형에서 잰다(모서리 상자는 가로·세로 중 화면을 덜 잃는 한 변으로 친다). inset 사각형이 8px 넘게 바뀌면 카메라가 멈춘 뒤 `refit()`. `runtime.test.ts`에 R3: 가짜 컨트롤러로 뷰포트를 바꾸면 `refit` 한 번, R4: inset이 4px 바뀌면 다시 맞추지 않음.
`debug.ts`: `view(spec, { instant })`는 `apply(cmd, !instant)`. `info()`에 `zoom`, `viewHeight`, `usable`(쓸 수 있는 화면 NDC), `targetNdc`(지금 명령의 맞출 점 투영 범위), `objects`(label·plan_drawing 물건마다 고체 상자 꼭짓점 투영으로 `inFrustum`, 화면 크기 `px`, 절개로 숨었는지 `hidden`).

> 빛 굽기로 바뀜. 시작하기 전에 「빛 굽기로 바꾸는 것」 표대로 이 본문을 고친다.

**Files:**
- Create: `ui/src/twin3d/scene/cutaway.ts`, `ui/tests/twin3d/cutaway.test.ts`
- Modify: `ui/src/twin3d/scene/TwinScene.ts`(`setView(view)`·바뀐 것만 적용), `ui/src/twin3d/TwinRuntime.ts`(방향이 바뀌거나 초점이 바뀌면 다시 계산), `ui/src/twin3d/debug.ts`(`info().wallSamples`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `cutAction`, `ceilingVisible`, `facesCamera`(Task 7), `Registry.wallPieces()`(Task 9, `kind`·`faces`·`rooms` 포함), `CameraController.heading()`(Task 10)
- Produces:

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 시험**

`ui/tests/twin3d/cutaway.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { cutaway, headingChanged, pieceUp, type WallPiece } from "../../src/twin3d/scene/cutaway";

const heading: [number, number] = [Math.SQRT1_2, Math.SQRT1_2]; // 카메라가 +x+z 쪽
const back: WallPiece = { key: "N", wall: "N", kind: "exterior", faces: [{ room: "A", normal: [0, 1] }], rooms: ["A"] };   // 안쪽 면이 카메라 쪽 = 뒤쪽 바깥벽
const front: WallPiece = { key: "S", wall: "S", kind: "exterior", faces: [{ room: "A", normal: [0, -1] }], rooms: ["A"] };
const inner: WallPiece = { key: "I#0", wall: "I", kind: "interior", faces: [{ room: "A", normal: [1, 0] }, { room: "B", normal: [-1, 0] }], rooms: ["A"] };
const innerB: WallPiece = { key: "I#1", wall: "I", kind: "interior", faces: [{ room: "B", normal: [1, 0] }], rooms: ["B"] };
const input = (over = {}) => ({ heading, focusRoom: null, focusCenter: null, mode: "auto" as const, showCeiling: false, ...over });

describe("벽 절개", () => {
  it("뒤쪽 바깥벽은 세우고 앞쪽 바깥벽·안벽은 낮춘다", () => {
    const r = cutaway([back, front, inner], [], input(), null, 1.05);
    expect([r.upper.get("N"), r.upper.get("S"), r.upper.get("I#0")]).toEqual([true, false, false]);
  });
  it("초점 방의 뒤쪽 벽은 그 방 구간만 세운다", () => {
    const r = cutaway([inner, innerB], [], input({ focusRoom: "A" }), null, 1.05);
    expect([r.upper.get("I#0"), r.upper.get("I#1")]).toEqual([true, false]);
  });
  it("히스테리시스: 내적 0.3은 세워져 있으면 그대로, 낮춰져 있으면 그대로", () => {
    const w: WallPiece = { ...back, faces: [{ room: "A", normal: [0.3 / Math.SQRT1_2 / 2, 0.3 / Math.SQRT1_2 / 2] }] };
    expect(pieceUp(w, input(), true)).toBe(true);
    expect(pieceUp(w, input(), false)).toBe(false);
  });
  it("낮춘 벽의 문은 자르고, 높은 벽 장식은 숨기고, 가전은 그대로", () => {
    const o = (id: string, category: string, mount: string, elevation: number) => ({ id, category, mount, room: "A", plan: { position: [0, 0], facing_deg: 0, elevation, wall: "S" }, states: {} }) as never;
    const r = cutaway([front], [o("door", "openings", "opening", 0), o("frame", "decor", "wall", 1.6), o("ac", "appliances", "wall", 1.95)], input(), null, 1.05);
    expect([r.objects.get("door"), r.objects.get("frame"), r.objects.get("ac")]).toEqual(["clip", "hide", "show"]);
    expect([...r.hiddenIds]).toEqual(["frame"]);
  });
  it("full은 모두 세우고 low는 모두 낮춘다", () => {
    expect(cutaway([front], [], input({ mode: "full" }), null, 1.05).upper.get("S")).toBe(true);
    expect(cutaway([back], [], input({ mode: "low" }), null, 1.05).upper.get("N")).toBe(false);
  });
  it("방 초점: 카메라 쪽 옆방의 높은 가구는 잘라 보기 높이에서 자르고, 뒤쪽·낮은 것·초점 방 것은 그대로", () => {
    const o = (id: string, room: string, x: number, height: number) => ({ id, category: "furniture", mount: "floor", room, height, plan: { position: [x, 0], facing_deg: 0, elevation: 0 }, states: {} }) as never;
    const objs = [o("wardrobe", "B", 6, 2.0), o("back", "C", -2, 2.0), o("table", "B", 6, 0.75), o("mine", "A", 2, 2.0)];
    const r = cutaway([], objs, input({ heading: [1, 0], focusRoom: "A", focusCenter: [2, 0] }), null, 1.05);
    expect(["wardrobe", "back", "table", "mine"].map((id) => r.objects.get(id))).toEqual(["clip", "show", "show", "show"]);
    expect(cutaway([], objs, input({ heading: [1, 0] }), null, 1.05).objects.get("wardrobe")).toBe("show");
  });
  it("방향이 1e-3보다 덜 돌면 다시 계산하지 않는다", () => {
    expect(headingChanged([1, 0], [Math.cos(0.0005), Math.sin(0.0005)])).toBe(false);
    expect(headingChanged([1, 0], [Math.cos(0.01), Math.sin(0.01)])).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`ui/src/twin3d/scene/cutaway.ts`
```ts
// Dynamic wall hiding: 나눈 벽 메시(아래 0~1.05m · 위 1.05~2.3m)의 위 조각만 숨긴다(cutaway). 투명·자르기 평면은 벽에 안 쓴다.
// 세우는 벽: 안쪽 면이 카메라를 보는 바깥벽(뒤쪽 벽), 초점 방의 뒤쪽 벽(그 방 구간만). 경계에서 흔들리지 않게 히스테리시스.
import type { Plan2 } from "../../../../src/contract/events.ts";
import type { SceneObject, WallFace } from "../../../../src/contract/scene.ts";
import { ceilingVisible, cutAction } from "./walls";

export interface WallPiece { key: string; wall: string; kind: "exterior" | "interior" | "solid"; faces: WallFace[]; rooms: string[] }
export interface CutawayInput { heading: Plan2; focusRoom: string | null; focusCenter: Plan2 | null; mode: "auto" | "full" | "low"; showCeiling: boolean }
export interface CutawayResult { upper: Map<string, boolean>; objects: Map<string, "show" | "hide" | "clip">; hiddenIds: Set<string> }
export const CUT_T = { raise: 0.35, lower: 0.25 } as const;

/** 방 초점일 때 카메라 쪽 옆방의 잘라 보기 높이를 넘는 물건인가. */
function blocksFocus(o: SceneObject & { height?: number }, input: CutawayInput, cut: number): boolean {
  if (!input.focusRoom || !input.focusCenter || !o.room || o.room === input.focusRoom) return false;
  const top = (o.mount === "floor" ? 0 : o.plan.elevation ?? 0) + (o.height ?? 0);
  const [px, py] = o.plan.position;
  return top > cut + 0.05 && (px - input.focusCenter[0]) * input.heading[0] + (py - input.focusCenter[1]) * input.heading[1] > 0;
}

const facing = (f: WallFace, h: Plan2) => f.normal[0] * h[0] + f.normal[1] * h[1];

export function pieceUp(piece: WallPiece, input: CutawayInput, prevUp: boolean | undefined, t = CUT_T): boolean {
  if (input.mode === "full") return true;
  if (input.mode === "low") return false;
  const need = prevUp ? t.lower : t.raise;
  const faces = piece.faces.filter((f) => !piece.rooms.length || piece.rooms.includes(f.room));
  if (piece.kind === "exterior" && faces.some((f) => facing(f, input.heading) > need)) return true;
  return Boolean(input.focusRoom) && faces.some((f) => f.room === input.focusRoom && facing(f, input.heading) > need);
}

export function cutaway(pieces: WallPiece[], objects: SceneObject[], input: CutawayInput, prev: CutawayResult | null, cut: number): CutawayResult {
  const upper = new Map(pieces.map((p) => [p.key, pieceUp(p, input, prev?.upper.get(p.key))]));
  const byWall = new Map<string, boolean>();
  for (const p of pieces) byWall.set(p.wall, (byWall.get(p.wall) ?? false) || upper.get(p.key)!);
  const objs = new Map<string, "show" | "hide" | "clip">();
  const hiddenIds = new Set<string>();
  for (const o of objects) {
    const action = cutAction(o, byWall, cut);
    const shown = ceilingVisible(o, { focus: input.focusRoom, showCeiling: input.showCeiling });
    const v = !shown || action === "hide" ? "hide" : action === "clip" || blocksFocus(o, input, cut) ? "clip" : "show";
    objs.set(o.id, v);
    if (v === "hide") hiddenIds.add(o.id);
  }
  return { upper, objects: objs, hiddenIds };
}

export function headingChanged(a: Plan2, b: Plan2, eps = 1e-3): boolean {
  return Math.abs(Math.atan2(a[0] * b[1] - a[1] * b[0], a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1])) > eps;
}
```
`TwinScene.setView(input)`: `cutaway(...)`을 지난 결과와 비교해 바뀐 조각만 적용한다. 위 조각을 숨길 때는 `visible=false`가 아니라 `layers.set(1)`(그 안 빛은 계속 비춤, 상태 효과 `visible`과 안 섞임), 보일 때 `layers.set(0)`. `clip`은 그 물건 재질에 `clippingPlanes = [new Plane(new Vector3(0, -1, 0), 바닥 + cut)]`(재질 복제 한 번). 바뀐 것이 있으면 `invalidate("walls")`. 그림자는 `rest` 뒤 프레임에서 다시 그린다(엔진이 "walls"를 그림자 이유로 가짐). `TwinRuntime`은 `onBeforeRender`에서 `headingChanged`일 때와 초점 방이 바뀔 때만 다시 계산한다(`focusCenter` = 그 방 `center`).
`debug.ts` `info().wallSamples`: 세운 벽 위 조각마다 카메라 쪽 면의 가운데(바닥 + 1.6m, 면에서 방 쪽 2cm)를 화면 픽셀로 투영하고, 그 점으로 쏜 레이의 첫 맞음이 그 벽인 점만 담는다(흰 벽 색 표본, P3 기준).

`ui/tests/twin3d/lighting.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { shadowFrustum } from "../../src/twin3d/render/lighting";

describe("그림자 상자", () => {
  it("빛 방향에서 본 집 꼭짓점을 딱 감싸고 여유를 둔다", () => {
    const corners = [[0, 0, 0], [4, 0, 0], [0, 0, 2], [4, 0, 2], [0, 2, 0], [4, 2, 0], [0, 2, 2], [4, 2, 2]] as [number, number, number][];
    const f = shadowFrustum(corners, [0, 1, 0], 0.5); // 바로 위에서
    expect(f.right - f.left).toBeCloseTo(5);
    expect(f.top - f.bottom).toBeCloseTo(3);
    expect(f.target).toEqual([2, 1, 1]);
    expect(f.near).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(f.far - f.near).toBeCloseTo(2 + 1);
  });
});
```

`ui/tests/twin3d/light-pool.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { DEFAULT_LIGHT_CAPS, allocateLights } from "../../src/twin3d/scene/light-pool";

const L = (id: string, kind: "spot" | "area" | "point", watts: number, ceiling = false) => ({ id, kind, watts, ceiling });

describe("빛 풀", () => {
  it("천장 조명 먼저, 그다음 센 빛", () => {
    const on = allocateLights([L("led", "point", 1), L("ceil", "point", 5, true), L("fridge", "point", 10)], { spot: 0, area: 0, point: 2, total: 2 });
    expect(on.size).toBe(2);
    expect([...on].sort()).toEqual(["ceil", "fridge"]);
  });
  it("종류별 자리와 전체 상한을 함께 지킨다", () => {
    const many = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => L(`s${i}`, "spot", i));
    expect(allocateLights(many, { spot: 8, area: 4, point: 6, total: 16 }).size).toBe(8);
    const mixed = [...many, ...Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, i) => L(`a${i}`, "area", 30 + i))];
    const on = allocateLights(mixed, DEFAULT_LIGHT_CAPS);
    expect([on.size, [...on].filter((id) => id.startsWith("a")).length]).toEqual([6, 2]);
  });
});
```

`ui/tests/twin3d/provenance.test.ts`
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { decorShown, isAgentTarget, isSelectable, provenanceNotes } from "../../src/twin3d/core/provenance";
import { PROVENANCE_NOTE } from "../../src/twin3d/ui/labels";

const p = (over: object) => ({ origin: "label", size: "label", facing: "inferred", height: "catalog", model: "builder", builder: null, ...over }) as never;

describe("출처 표시", () => {
  it("실제 라벨 배치는 L3에 덧말 없음, L4에 방향 추정", () => {
    expect(provenanceNotes(p({}), 3)).toEqual([]);
    expect(provenanceNotes(p({}), 4)).toEqual(["inferredFacing"]);
  });
  it("대체 모양·도면 기준·옮긴 자리·임시 모델·출처 없음", () => {
    expect(provenanceNotes(p({ model: "substitute" }), 3)).toEqual(["substitute"]);
    expect(provenanceNotes(p({ model: "substitute" }), 4)).toEqual(["substitute", "inferredFacing"]);
    expect(provenanceNotes(p({ origin: "plan_drawing", facing: "manual" }), 3)).toEqual(["plan_drawing"]);
    expect(provenanceNotes(p({ origin: "manual", facing: "manual" }), 3)).toEqual(["manual"]);
    expect(provenanceNotes(p({ model: "placeholder" }), 3)).toEqual(["placeholder"]);
    expect(provenanceNotes(null, 3)).toEqual(["unknown"]);
  });
  it("장면 전체에 출처가 없으면(옛 산출물) 덧말을 붙이지 않는다", () => expect(provenanceNotes(null, 3, false)).toEqual([]));
  it("모든 열쇠에 화면 글이 있다", () => {
    for (const k of ["substitute", "plan", "plan_drawing", "manual", "runtime", "placeholder", "inferredFacing", "unknown"] as const) expect(PROVENANCE_NOTE[k]).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it("장식은 고를 수 없고 에이전트 대상도 아니며 장식 층이 꺼지면 안 보인다", () => {
    const d = p({ origin: "decor" });
    expect([isSelectable(d), isAgentTarget(d), decorShown(d, false), decorShown(d, true)]).toEqual([false, false, false, true]);
    expect(decorShown(p({}), false)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```
